## Phase 82: QA & Verification Sweep (ultracode)

<!-- gate-log:phase-82 direction=approved delivery=accepted -->

Decision: [[qa-verification-sweep]] (high). Coverage-defined QA sweep over nana-dev-kit, 8 audit areas
(wiring/firing/companions/schema/drift/coverage/docs/usage), four stages: T1 controls-first baseline
(clean-on-seed = instrument-dead) -> T2 ultracode fan-out of 8 READ-ONLY candidate-generator agents
(subagents NEVER produce evidence — Jake's A2 reject) -> T3 orchestrator-only verdicts + mid-phase
findings report BEFORE any fix + serialized fixes under the pre-registered boundary -> T4 verification
matrix close-out. Zero confirmed defects is a valid success — the deliverable is the matrix.
READ-ONLY: .dev-wiki/assumption-ledger.md, eval/amplifier/**, eval/assumption-screen/**.
First live dogfood of the Phase-81 assumption-approval gate (A1 don't-know -> usage area added;
A2 reject -> orchestrator-only verdicts; ledger block appended, 6 rows, all_accept:false).

## Phase 94 — Clean Consumer Memory Re-measure

<!-- gate-log:phase-94 direction=approved delivery=accepted -->

Standard ceremony. Direction gate closed 2026-06-20 (ledger Phase-94; all_accept:false — A1/A4 accept, A2 don't-know→down-scope+expansion, A3→maintainer revised n=1 to n=3). <!-- gate-log:phase-99 direction=approved delivery=pending -->
